HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001","root_000709/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2370916cd1977ad62fcc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:9:1\",\"root_000709\",[\"B002\"]],[\"88:9:2\",\"root_000569\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çaba, sahibinin geriye dönüp değerli bulabildiği bir sonuçla ölçülür; önceki emeğin yalnız yorgunluk üretmesine karşı burada yapılan iş kabul edilebilir bir karşılık olarak geri döner.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003","root_000709/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3fb27efc40cc3cbef0df","text":"2. **Purposeful movement arrives at a place that fits.** Evidence: `س ع ي` B001 purposeful going/brisk movement; `ر ض و` B003 mutual consent; later `ج نن` B003 garden and `ن ع م` B011 agreeing place. Structural cue: movement language is followed by location language: `في جنة عالية`. Role: `سعي` becomes a path toward residence; `رضا` marks the match between traveler and destination. Abductive move: moral motion produces spatial belonging. Reading change: the ayah reads as arrival into a place consented to by both deed and person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_56e5497a5a06ad4184d2","text":"2. **Striving becomes an upward trajectory rather than static labor.** **Lexical:** `س ع ي` B001, purposeful going toward a desired end. **Cue:** `سعي` is followed by a `جَنَّةٍ عَالِيَةٍ` in 88:10 and `سُرُرٌ مَّرْفُوعَةٌ` in 88:13, with a spring and placed cups making the route materially viable. **Role:** purposeful motion supplies the trajectory; the repeated elevations supply its spatialized end. **Abduction:** I infer that textual succession diagrams movement from effort to elevation. **Reading change:** `سعيها` can be read as directed travel whose destination is an ordered high habitat, not action without a course.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B004","root_000709/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_627bfe0886ecfabad00b","text":"3. **Administrative endeavor contrasted with coercive control.** Evidence: `س ع ي` B003 agency/office over people, B004 denunciation to authority; `ر ض و` B004 appeasing another; later `س ط ر` B003 control is negated for the messenger. Structural cue: the surah later defines the messenger as reminder, not controller. Role: this reading keeps an administrative register active but limits it: the person’s own endeavor satisfies; the messenger does not coerce the outcome. Abductive move: final satisfaction must arise from recorded agency, not external domination. Reading change: `سعيها` reads as personally accountable action in a non-coercive warning economy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B004","root_000709/B003","root_000709/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_63a23ec0eee8ada01f76","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:9:1\",\"root_000709\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]],[\"88:9:2\",\"root_000569\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Vekillik, görev ve makama bildirme çağrışımları, memnuniyeti dışarıdan zorlanan uyumdan ayırıp kişinin kendi kayıt altına alınabilir eylemine bağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7dc6df74e996dbbbb5cf","text":"The ending `لست عليهم بمصيطر` prevented this ayah from being read as imposed compliance: satisfaction belongs to the face’s own `سعي`. The final `حسابهم` strengthened the record/account side of endeavor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8028870515bc4988079b","text":"88:10-14 progressively materialized the return on striving. High garden and raised couches made `س ع ي` B001 directional, while flowing water and placed cups made `ر ض و` B003's reciprocal fit unexpectedly concrete: the result agrees with the actor's bodily needs.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B001","root_000709/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8873ad7b7bf869017912","text":"1. **Endeavor received as satisfying result.** Evidence: `س ع ي` B002 work/earning/active endeavor; `ر ض و` B001 acceptance opposed to displeasure. Structural cue: this follows the softened faces and precedes garden placement. Role: effort is the cause-side, satisfaction the response-side. Abductive move: the face’s ease is not random; it corresponds to a remembered course of action. Reading change: the ayah reads as fulfilled endeavor, where effort returns to its owner as acceptance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b330c141f3b327255e17","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:9:2\",\"root_000569\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karşılıklı rıza çağrışımı, eylem, eyleyen ve ona verilen çevre arasında çift yönlü bir uygunluk kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cb42bd7a8a66206c88d5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:9:1\",\"root_000709\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çabanın amaçlı bir gidiş olması, yüksek bahçe ve yükseltilmiş sedirlerle birleşerek işi durağan bir uğraş değil, düzenli bir yere varan yukarı yönlü yolculuk gibi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000709/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_db2230877a9f1400e6d9","text":"1. **Endeavor is evaluated by a result the subject can accept.** **Lexical:** `س ع ي` B002, \"work, earning, and active endeavor,\" and `ر ض و` B001, acceptance opposed to displeasure. **Cue:** the possessive `سَعْيِهَا` is governed by `لِـ` and paired with `رَاضِيَةٌ`; it follows the failed return of 88:4-7 and precedes a fully provisioned setting in 88:10-14. **Role:** endeavor supplies the invested action, and satisfaction supplies its retrospective evaluation. **Abduction:** I infer that the garden's provisions are the endeavor's return; the ayah only states satisfaction in relation to striving. **Reading change:** the line reads as outcome-accounting: unlike the labor that terminates in weariness, this effort is recognizable to its agent as worthwhile.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000569/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ec380e04930a9237a1df","text":"3. **Satisfaction is reciprocal fit between action, agent, and environment.** **Lexical:** `ر ض و` B003, \"going along by mutual consent,\" alongside B001 acceptance. **Cue:** the feminine agent is pleased \"for/with\" its own endeavor, and the next five ayat arrange garden, quiet, water, couches, and cups around it. **Role:** the reciprocal branch models an agreement: the action yields a world that, in turn, agrees with the actor's needs. **Abduction:** I infer reciprocity across the pronoun and environmental sequence; only one grammatical experiencer of satisfaction is explicit. **Reading change:** `راضية` becomes more than an inward mood: it marks a successful fit among what was done, who did it, and the world received.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
